### pkg/ and cmd/

- `pkg/adapter/checkpoint.go`, `pkg/adapter/resume.go`, `pkg/adapter/server.go`, `pkg/adapter/coordination.go`, `pkg/adapter/oplock.go`, `pkg/adapter/slot.go`, `pkg/adapter/slotsession.go`: C1 (streaming produce and restore handlers; `Checkpoint`, `CoordinatorFence`, and `CheckpointBarrier` routed through the existing `useSlot` / `checkSlotSession` / per-slot-root pattern, because the shipped pod-global session gate can never pass on a `maxConcurrentSessions > 1` pod (`pkg/adapter/session.go:234-236`) and `checkpointRoots()` resolves the pod-global workspace root; the fence's `lastFenced` / `initialized` state moved from the pod-global `coordinationState` (`coordination.go:26-34`) onto the per-slot state, and `Checkpoint` and `Resume` gated on the request's `coordination_generation` against that state, which is the gate the shipped adapter carries on `CheckpointBarrier` alone (`coordination.go:143-159`) and without which a displaced coordinator's `Checkpoint` is accepted by the pod and holds its op-lock; the fence-driven cancellation the restated `spec/10:36` mandates, under which the `Checkpoint` and `Resume` handlers register a per-slot cancel func and `CoordinatorFence` invokes every cancel func of a superseded generation before it records the new generation and acknowledges, so the victim's handler returns and releases the pod-global op-lock (`server.go:328`, `oplock.go:57-73`) before the new coordinator's checkpoint contends for it; `CheckpointSink`, `CheckpointSource`, and `CheckpointAborter` deleted; barrier flush reduced to quiesce-and-ack; trigger label and storage-failure metric moved to the gateway), C7 (the adapter's SIGTERM path calls `NotifyPodTerminating` and blocks on it ahead of every teardown step, including `lifecycle.Close()`, so the runtime's quiesce channel outlives the gateway-driven `Checkpoint`).
- `cmd/lenny-adapter/main.go`, `cmd/lenny-adapter/prestop.go`, `cmd/runtimes/echo-embedded/main.go`, `cmd/runtimes/preconnect-echo/main.go`: C1 (drop the sink and restorer fields; wire `CheckpointMetrics`, `CheckpointEvents`, `WorkspaceSize`, `WorkspaceSizeLimitBytes`, and the pool and level labels), C7 (the SIGTERM path calls `NotifyPodTerminating` and blocks on it **before** `ShutdownDemoteSDK` (`main.go:397`), before `lifecycle.Close()` (`:398-400`), and before `srv.GracefulStop()` (`:401`), so the intra-pod lifecycle channel is still open for the runtime quiesce the gateway-driven `Checkpoint` needs; the adapter reads its grace period from `LENNY_TERMINATION_GRACE_SECONDS` to compute `budget_ms`, and bounds its wait on the RPC so that `LENNY_DEMOTE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS + 5s` of the budget remains for the §6.1 demote-and-exit steps. `prestop.go`'s `preStopDeps` `{signal, alive, sleep, logf}` and `runPreStop` are **unchanged**, because the notification lives in the adapter process rather than in the exec. Two new subcommands are added beside `prestop`: `prestop-wait`, the wait-only hook the sidecar `runtime` container runs, which polls the adapter's existing listener on `127.0.0.1:50051` across the shared pod network namespace and returns when it stops accepting, signalling nothing and never calling `syscall.Kill`; and `install-drain-wait`, which the initContainer runs to copy its own executable into the `drain-bin` volume, since the distroless adapter image carries no shell and no `cp`).
- `pkg/controller/sandbox/podspec/podspec.go`: C7 (`defaultTerminationGraceSeconds` (`:75`) rises from 120s to 180s; the sidecar `runtime` container (`:562-573`), which carries no `Lifecycle` today and whose process would otherwise be SIGTERMed at t=0, gains a **wait-only** preStop hook running the staged `/lenny/drain/lenny-adapter prestop-wait`, plus a read-only mount of a new `drain-bin` `emptyDir` (the sidecar-only `runtimeMounts` literal at `:509-518`; the volume itself is appended to the sidecar builder's slice at `:493`, **not** inside `podVolumes()` at `:823-859`, which takes no `Inputs` and is called by both layouts at `:493` and `:592`) that a new initContainer on the sidecar layout stages the adapter binary into. `preStopDrainHook` (`:1229`) is **not** reused there: its `lenny-adapter prestop` command is absent from the third-party `Runtime.spec.image` (`:236-237`) and `runPreStop` signals its own container's PID 1, which in that container is the runtime process (`cmd/lenny-adapter/prestop.go:12-16`, `:99-101`), and `shareProcessNamespace` stays unset (`podspec.go:485-487`, `spec/13:16-23`). `preStopDrainHook` and its two existing call sites (`:560`, `:645`), its exec command, its `--timeout`, and the 10s `preStopDrainMarginSeconds` (`:82`) are unchanged, and the embedded layout gains neither the volume nor the initContainer. `LENNY_TERMINATION_GRACE_SECONDS` is rendered on the sidecar `adapter` container (`:551`) and the embedded container (`:638`) beside the existing `POD_NAME`; `terminationGrace` (`:1193-1220`) keeps its flat default, its precedence order, and its preConnect floor, and its §6.1 grace-sizing comment (`:1203-1212`) is restated on the new SIGTERM sequence, including the demote-and-exit reserve the S3 floor now carries as an additive term). `pkg/controller/sandbox/controller_test.go` (`:347`) is touched with it: it asserts the rendered pod's 120s grace period and is the only assertion of the default outside `podspec/`.
- `pkg/admission/pool_config_validator/validator.go`: S3 and C7 (`decideTerminationBudget` (`:585-660`, its doc comment as well as its body) computes, for a session-mode pool, `maxConcurrentSessions × (max(MaxTieredCheckpointCapSeconds(workspaceSizeLimitBytes), 90) + postgresFallbackBudgetSeconds) + preStopDrainMarginSeconds + sdkDemoteReserveSeconds` (`:635`), and for a service-mode pool `preStopDrainMarginSeconds + sdkDemoteReserveSeconds` alone: the per-slot term is floored at the 90s tier the gateway must select for a session with no prior checkpoint and gains the 60s Postgres fallback, the sum drops the `checkpointBarrierAckTimeoutSeconds` term as a gateway-drain deadline, replaces `minStreamDrainSeconds` (30) with `preStopDrainMarginSeconds` (10), and adds the `sdkDemoteTimeoutSeconds + sdkDemoteGraceMarginSeconds` demote-and-exit reserve (10s), which is serial with the gateway's checkpoint window rather than inside it. The service-mode arm carries neither checkpoint term, because such a pool materializes no workspace (`spec/05:391`) and its claimless session (`pkg/gateway/sessionserver/create.go:326`) leaves `ListActiveByPod` with nothing to checkpoint on its terminating pod, so charging it `maxConcurrent × 150s` would reject a shipped, currently-admitted configuration on a path the PoolScalingController cannot bypass. `effectiveMaxConcurrent` (`:452-462`) returns `sessionPolicy.maxConcurrentSessions` for a session-mode pool instead of collapsing to 1, which is the only class that runs the serialized per-slot eviction checkpoints. The floor is compared against the pool's **effective** grace period: the `spec.TerminationGracePeriodSeconds != nil` guard (`:644`) is dropped and a new `defaultAgentTerminationGraceSeconds` constant mirrors the podspec's 180s default. Five doc sites state the retired formula or the retired mode rule and are restated with the code, because the §6 tier-11 assertion compares all of them against the spec: the function's formula block (`:591-600`), its rejection-rule bullets (`:602-612`), the **package doc comment** (`:16-25`), which restates the retired floor verbatim and asserts that "a session-mode pool uses a multiplier of 1", `DecideTemplate`'s doc comment (`:406-411`), which restates the same formula and the same collapse, and `DecideTemplate`'s inline budget rationale (`:437-444`), which asserts the rule "is not subject to the executionMode the deployer happened to pick". The file's edit range therefore covers `:1-35`, `:396-446`, `:452-462`, and `:585-660`. The `422 INVALID_POOL_CONFIGURATION` rejection, its metric, the BarrierAck-floor rule (the check at `:628-634`, doc bullet `:609-612`), the >600s warning, the `maxTerminationGracePeriodSeconds` hard-ceiling rejection, and the rule's scope over every write are unchanged; the rejection message names the new terms, the effective value, and the pool field the deployer must raise).
- `pkg/admission/webhook`: C7 (`TestPoolConfigValidatorPropagatesTerminationGraceWarning_spec_5_2_516` (`pool_config_validator_test.go:191`) applies a **service-mode** `maxConcurrent: 8` template with no `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` and asserts it is admitted with an "840s" warning; under the service-mode arm its floor is 20s, which the 180s effective default clears and which sits below the 600s warning threshold, so the case flips from admit-with-one-warning to admit-with-no-warning and is rewritten onto a session-mode template with `sessionPolicy.maxConcurrentSessions: 8`, whose 1220s floor keeps the warning the test pins. `TestPoolConfigValidatorRejectsTerminationGraceCeilingBreach_spec_5_2_516` (`pool_config_validator_test.go:215`) applies the same service-mode `maxConcurrent: 8` template with `maxTerminationGracePeriodSeconds: 600` and asserts the ceiling rejection its shipped 840s floor produces; the 20s service-mode floor clears the ceiling, so the template is admitted and its rejection, its 422, and its `spec.maxTerminationGracePeriodSeconds (600s)` message assertions all invert. It is re-keyed onto a session-mode template with `sessionPolicy.maxConcurrentSessions: 8`, whose 1220s floor breaches the same ceiling, and keeps every assertion. The webhook handler itself is unchanged).
- `pkg/gateway/storage/leasestore/leasestore.go`, `pkg/gateway/storage/leasestore/failover.go`, `pkg/gateway/storage/leasestore/pgstore/pgstore.go`: C7 (a `Steal` primitive on the `LeaseStore` interface (`:58-66`) beside `Acquire` (`:125-141`), which returns `ErrHeld` against a live holder and therefore cannot serve the termination-path handoff. All three implementations carry it, because all three carry compile-time interface assertions (`leasestore.go:67`, `failover.go:39`, `pgstore/pgstore.go:50`) and production hands the handler the `*Failover` (`cmd/lenny-gateway/stores.go:1272`) rather than the Redis store: a Redis Lua compare-and-set on `*Store`, a `pg_advisory_xact_lock`-guarded unconditional `UPDATE` returning the displaced holder on `pgstore.Store` (the §12.4 durable fallback `spec/12:217` mandates for the lease role), and primary-then-fallback routing on `*Failover`. `Steal` is called only by the `NotifyPodTerminating` handler, and every other caller keeps `Acquire` and its `ErrHeld` stand-down (`pkg/gateway/coordination/coordination/coordination.go:132-137`). The package doc's "a lease can never be stolen" invariant (`leasestore.go:13-14`) names the termination-path exception. `Release` is already a no-op for a non-holder (`:170-172`) and `Renew` (`:149-151`) has no coordination-path caller, so neither changes; the displaced coordinator stands down through the sweeper's own `Acquire`/`ErrHeld` arm, which is unchanged and carries no registry unbind, because it fires for every session another replica holds the lease for rather than only for a displaced one).
- `pkg/apis/lenny/v1alpha1/sandboxtemplate_types.go`: S3 (the `TerminationGracePeriodSeconds` (`:167-178`), `MaxTerminationGracePeriodSeconds` (`:160-165`), `WorkspaceSizeLimitBytes` (`:180-190`), and `CheckpointBarrierAckTimeoutSeconds` (`:192-202`) doc comments carry the corrected formula and the 180s default base; the last one drops its "adds it to the per-pod `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` floor" clause and keeps the BarrierAck-floor rule it still governs. `SessionPolicy` (`:28-34`) gains `MaxConcurrentSessions *int32`, the multiplier the grace floor names and the field `spec/04:416` already requires the CRD to validate (`sessionPolicy.maxConcurrentSessions >= 1`) but which the Go type omits, so the webhook can read it; `spec/04:416` and the §4.6.3 ownership row at `spec/04:592` (`SandboxTemplate.spec.*` → PoolScalingController) are confirmed unchanged, because both already cover the field). This is the controller-gen source of the chart CRD's field docs, which `make generate` re-renders and which `pkg/embedded/crds/lenny.dev_sandboxtemplates.yaml` mirrors byte-for-byte.
- `pkg/controller/poolscaling/poolstoresource.go`: S3 (the CRD write path, which is `sessionPolicyToCRD` (`:145-159`, called at `:89-90`) rather than the `PoolConfig` assignment at `:124-130`. `sessionPolicyToCRD` returns nil unless the store row carries a recycle block with a scrub profile or the microvm acknowledgment, so an ordinary non-recycling pool renders `spec.sessionPolicy = nil` and the webhook can read no multiplier however the Go type changes. It now emits a non-nil `SessionPolicy` carrying `MaxConcurrentSessions` whenever the store row sets it, independently of the recycle guards, and its doc comment is restated. `toConfig` (`:65-96`) additionally mirrors the new `termination_grace_period_seconds` pool column onto `SandboxTemplateSpec.TerminationGracePeriodSeconds`, which is the only writer of the field the grace floor is compared against: without it the field is nil on every template the PSC applies, the effective grace period is always the 180s default, and the dropped `!= nil` guard would reject every `maxConcurrentSessions ≥ 2` pool with no remediation path. The poolstore stays the source of truth and no scaling behaviour changes).
- `pkg/gateway/runtime/poolstore`, `pkg/gateway/externalapi/admin/pools.go` (`PoolPayload` `:91-92`, `UpdatePoolRequest` `:237-238`), and `migrations/`: S3 (a nullable `termination_grace_period_seconds` column on the pools table, the field on `poolstore.Pool` and its pgstore, and the admin-API pool create and update payloads, which is the plumbing the deployer-facing `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` documented at `docs/operator-guide/configuration.md:285` and mandated by `spec/05:542` ("Deployers must set `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` accordingly") has never had. An unset column leaves the CRD field nil and the pool on the 180s effective default. The migration is asserted by a new `prodMigrationSchema` row in `tests/tier2_component/migrations/prod_columns_test.go`, without which `scripts/lint-migrations.sh:87-88` fails the build for a migration number no test references, and the admin-API and store round-trip are asserted at tier 2, because that path is the only remediation for the newly fail-closed `422`).
- `pkg/gateway/coordination/coordination/coordination.go`: **no edit.** The sweeper's `ErrHeld` stand-down arm (`:132-137`) is listed here to record that it was examined and left alone. It is the generic "another replica holds this lease" arm rather than a displacement signal, because the sweeper races to `Acquire` every non-terminal session of every tenant (`:109`, `:133`) while the `podsession.Registry` binding is written by whichever replica served start or resume (`start.go:2232`, `:3150`), so a registry unbind on that arm would drop live bindings for healthy sessions the replica is serving (C7).
- `pkg/gateway/session/sessionstore` (`pgstore/pgstore.go:692`, `memstore`): C7 (a `ListActiveByPod` selector beside the existing `GetActiveSlotsByPod`, on the same `idx_sessions_active_by_pod` partial index, so the `NotifyPodTerminating` handler resolves the terminating pod's sessions from Postgres rather than from a process-local registry).
- `pkg/gateway/metrics/gatewaymetrics`: C2 (`lenny_checkpoint_partial_total` is registered with `pool` alone and has no caller, so it is widened to the `spec/16:195` label set (`pool`, `recovered`, `manifest_reason`, `trigger`) and given its emitter on the `partial = true` finalisation, with the `recovered` flip on the resume that reassembles the row; `lenny_checkpoint_barrier_ack_total{pool, outcome}` and `lenny_checkpoint_barrier_ack_duration_seconds{pool}` are **registered for the first time** in `gatewaymetrics_checkpoint.go` and emitted from `barrier.Coordinator` (both exist today only as name-and-type rows in `pkg/observability/metrics/catalog.go:127-128`, which registers nothing, so the barrier success rate `spec/16:41` rests on is uncomputable); `lenny_gc_tombstones_pruned_total`'s `table` value `partial_manifest` becomes `checkpoint_manifest`; `IncCheckpointPartialManifestsSuperseded` (`gatewaymetrics.go:408-412`), whose registration carries `pool` and whose helper has **no caller anywhere in the tree**, gets its emitter in the upload driver's supersede step, one increment per soft-deleted `partial = true` row and none on the two arms where the predicate aborts the reservation instead of superseding; `IncCheckpointStorageFailure` (`gatewaymetrics.go:608-613`) gains a `reason` parameter instead of hard-coding `retry_exhausted`, so the degraded barrier dispatch emits `manifest_write_failed` and the zero-chunk fall-through emits `no_chunks_committed`; the counter's four registered labels, including `reason`, are otherwise unchanged and `spec/16:200` is corrected to declare the full value set the four shipped emitters and these two conditions produce; and `lenny_prestop_barrier_target_source_total` (`gatewaymetrics_podlifecycle.go:142-144`, `IncPreStopBarrierTargetSource` at `gatewaymetrics.go:1278-1282`) is **unchanged** on the Go side: it is the third of the three spec-versus-code label disagreements the §6 tier-11 parity gate covers, and S1 reconciles it on `spec/16:44` and on `docs/reference/metrics.md:40` by dropping `pool` from both declarations, because the counter is emitted once per fan-out across every pool the draining replica coordinates (`barrier.go:164-166`). The `pool` value the barrier-ack counter and its histogram need comes from the new `Pool` field on `barrier.Target`, which `MirrorTargetLister.Targets` and the cache `Fallback` lister populate (S1)).
- `pkg/gateway/checkpoint/checkpointer/checkpointer.go` and `partialcleanup.go`: C2 (`Sweep` (`:173-178`), the serial one-goroutine periodic producer, becomes a bounded worker pool sized by `gateway.checkpointWorker.concurrency`, whose semaphore is the single replica-wide bound the seal, the barrier flush, and the eviction producers share, without which a serialized sweep of `maxSessionsPerReplica` sessions cannot finish inside `periodicCheckpointIntervalSeconds` at the §4.4 SLO and `CheckpointStale` fires permanently at Tier 3; `Run` (`:122-133`) is unchanged apart from constructing the pool; upload driver with a second entry point for an already-minted `checkpoint_id` and an already-committed intent row, which the barrier path and the C7 eviction handler both reserve through, `PartialChunkDeleter.DeleteByPrefix` replaced by the per-key derived-key delete, per-`(session_id, slot_id)` single-flight lock, gateway-minted `checkpoint_id`, the stale-binding check in the reservation (the transaction that reads the session row's `coordination_generation` also reads its `pod_assignment` and compares it against the binding's `SandboxName`, dropping the binding through `Registry.Remove` and returning `ErrNoBinding` without writing anything when they disagree, and the abort path drops it when the `Checkpoint` RPC fails because the pod is gone, which is the only reclaimer a destroyed pod's registry bindings have), counter reservation before each chunk `Put`, storage-quota reservation from `CheckpointStart.probed_bytes`, tenant-scoped chunk `Put` through the cataloged store, the driver-owned MinIO retry ladder over the context-carrying store surface, the generation-fenced supersede, finalising UPDATE, `WorkspaceSnapshot.Bytes` from `CheckpointSummary` on the `partial = false` arm only, no session-record stamp and no retention INSERT on the `partial = true` arm, and `recordRetention` consuming `Rotate`'s returned records to delete the rotated-out checkpoint's chunks and soft-delete its manifest row), C4 (re-key the cleaner and re-scope `PartialCleaner.CleanupAfterResume` to `partial = TRUE AND deleted_at IS NULL`), C5 (`snapshotWithTrigger`, `triggerForSource` deleted, the tier cap and the eviction retry budget stated as two ceilings on one attempt), C7 (the `partial = false` finalisation deletes the session's `session_eviction_state` row as a best-effort post-finalisation write).
- `pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/prestop/prestop.go` and `prestop/checkpoint_adapter.go`: C2 (`fireBarrier` stays the bounded fan-out caller of the barrier coordinator), C5 (`CheckpointFn` routes through `snapshotWithTrigger` with `checkpoint.TriggerEviction` and passes the per-session tier-cap budget as `checkpoint_timeout_at`). C7 does **not** reach this package: the eviction-fallback writer is invoked by the `NotifyPodTerminating` handler and is structurally unreachable from the gateway's own drain.
- New restore-driver package under `pkg/gateway/checkpoint`: C2 (loads the manifest row named on the request, derives the chunk keys, `Get`s them in index order, pushes them to the adapter over the client-streaming `Resume` RPC, falls back to the newest active `partial = false` row strictly below the failed row through `SelectLatestFullBefore` on a contiguity or reassembly failure, so a broken newest full checkpoint resolves the previous retained one instead of itself, cleans up a `partial = true` row afterwards, and reports the mode it actually took and the recovery fraction it computed).
- `GatewayControl.NotifyPodTerminating` handler on the gateway, implemented as a method on `leasecontrol.Service` (`pkg/gateway/mcpfabric/delegationtree/leasecontrol/leasecontrol.go:390-395`), which is the **only** registered `adapterv1.GatewayControlServer` in the tree (`cmd/lenny-gateway/main.go:700`, built by `newGatewayControlServer` at `:582` and composed at `cmd/lenny-gateway/controlserver.go:161`) and which embeds `UnimplementedGatewayControlServer`, so a handler registered anywhere else would answer `codes.Unimplemented` in the composed binary: C7 (the method delegates to a new eviction-checkpoint component passed in as one more `leasecontrol` seam beside `scrubReports`, and the existing interceptor chain at `main.go:690-693` already covers the new unary RPC; the component resolves the pod's bound sessions from `sessions.pod_assignment` through `ListActiveByPod`, because the adapter dials the load-balanced Service and the RPC lands on an arbitrary replica; dials the terminating pod's adapter through the new exported `podsession.Binder.AdoptForCheckpoint` and binds it for every session it holds no `podsession.Registry` binding for, and assumes coordination of every session bound to the pod through the §10.1 handoff (a `leasestore.Steal` of the coordination lease when this replica does not already hold it, and in every case the `coordination_generation` CAS **and the reservation of that slot's eviction checkpoint in the same transaction as the CAS**, then the `CoordinatorFence` over that connection), which fences any upload still in flight on any replica, so the new attempt's strictly-higher generation admits the supersede; the CAS and the fence are unconditional because a session's periodic uploads are driven by the replica holding its pod binding rather than by its lease holder (`checkpointer.go:174-176`, `:236-239`), so a handler that skipped them when it already held the lease would meet a binding-holding replica's live row at equal generation and abort on the settled-row term. The reservation is inside the handoff because the generation excludes only the peer's in-flight upload and not its next attempt, which re-reads the post-CAS value and presents the generation the pod's entry gate admits: the committed intent row is what a peer's later reservation aborts on, at equal generation, through the settled-row term, and it carries the pod-declared `budget_ms` deadline as its `checkpoint_timeout_at` so a slot reserved now and executed several minutes later is not readable as abandoned. Step 3 then executes the pre-minted attempts and mints, supersedes, and INSERTs nothing. The bind gate and the `Steal` gate are separate because the binding and the lease are held by uncorrelated replicas (`start.go:2232`, `:3150` versus the sweeper's `Acquire` race at `coordination.go:109`, `:133-137`), so a single lease-keyed gate would leave the replica that happens to hold the lease with no binding and fail every slot in `Checkpointer.snapshot` with `ErrNoBinding` (`checkpointer.go:236-239`); removes every binding for the terminating pod from its own registry, including one that was already there, and closes every client it dialed before it returns, since `PodExecutor.Release` (`pod.go:313`, the only `Registry.Remove` caller in the tree) runs on the session's teardown rather than on the loss of its pod, and a binding naming a destroyed pod would make `Checkpointer.Sweep` reserve a checkpoint per tick against a pod that cannot answer; the bindings other replicas hold for the same pod are retired by the C2 reservation's stale-binding check, which compares the binding's Sandbox name against the session's `pod_assignment` and drops the binding without writing anything when they disagree, and by the abort path when the `Checkpoint` RPC fails because the pod is gone; drives one eviction-trigger checkpoint per slot in slot-ID order under each slot's Stage 2 tier cap and the pod-declared `budget_ms`; invokes `evictionfallback.Writer` on a context detached from the slot's tier cap when a slot's MinIO retries are exhausted or its handoff cannot be taken, both of which fire while the slot's `postgresFallbackBudgetSeconds` is still inside the grace period the S3 floor sized; returns at `budget_ms` with the drain margin and the demote-and-exit reserve intact when the pod's budget elapses with slots unsettled, and completes those slots' fallback records and any total-loss `session.lost` publish afterwards on a gateway-owned context detached from the pod's lifetime, under the §4.4 60-second Postgres budget; and returns the per-slot outcome. No gateway-to-gateway forwarding RPC is added).
- `pkg/gateway/checkpoint/checkpointretention/checkpointretention.go`: S2 and C2 (`Record.Ref` carries `object_key_prefix`; `Rotate` keeps its Postgres-only contract and its returned records are consumed by `recordRetention`, which is where the rotated-out chunk delete and the manifest-row soft-delete are built).
- `pkg/gateway/storage/retentiongc/retentiongc.go` and `cmd/lenny-gateway/workers.go` (`:665-670`): C2 (the terminal-session sweep keeps `SoftDeleteSession` for the chunk objects and their `artifact_store` rows and gains a companion `checkpoint_manifest` deleter in the same `arts` slice).
- `pkg/gateway/storage/evictionfallback/evictionfallback.go` and `pkg/gateway/storage/evictionstatestore/evictionstatestore.go`: C7 (`Writer` gets its first non-test call site; `Delete` (`:234`) gets its first non-test caller, from the `partial = false` post-finalisation step, which first reads `last_message_context` and soft-deletes the eviction-context object through the tenant-scoped `ArtifactStore` when the context exceeded 2 KB, so the `spec/04:282` object-before-row ordering holds on the new trigger as well as on terminal-state cleanup. Neither type's stored semantics change: `WorkspaceLost` stays forced true by construction, which is correct on the one path that now reaches it).
- `pkg/gateway/quota/storagequota`: C2 (the checkpoint upload driver becomes a second `Reserve` caller alongside `pkg/gateway/sessionserver/upload.go:724`; no signature change).
- `pkg/gateway/externalapi/openapi/openapi.json`: C2 (the `Session` schema (`:110-136`) gains `failureReason` beside the closed `failureClass` enum, which is otherwise unchanged. The document is hand-authored rather than generated, so the field is added at its source).
- `pkg/gateway/checkpoint/partialmanifeststore/` (`partialmanifeststore.go`, `pgstore/pgstore.go`, `MemoryStore`): C4 (unified `checkpoint_manifest` record and store surface, re-keyed on `(tenant_id, checkpoint_id)`, the new `SelectForResume`, `SelectLatestFullBefore`, and `ListSweepable` selectors, `LatestActive` re-scoped to `partial = TRUE`, erasure contract preserved).
- `pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go` and `start.go`: C2 (`resumeOnPod` gates the restore path on `SelectForResume` rather than on `sessions.workspace_snapshot_ref`, and passes the selected `checkpoint_id` on `podsession.ResumeRequest`; `SessionResponse` (`sessionserver.go:2021-2032`) gains `FailureReason string \`json:"failureReason,omitempty"\`` and `toResponse` (`:3141-3172`) copies it off the row, because the §15.1 envelope carries only `failureClass` today and `WORKSPACE_SNAPSHOT_MISSING` has no class of its own, so the code `spec/25:4095` requires the gateway to surface would reach no client), C6 (`PartialManifestLookup` deleted at all four of its sites, `classifyResumeWithAdapter` classifies from the restore driver's `RestoreMode`, `emitResumedEvent` widens to carry `RecoveryFraction`, `resumeOnPod` writes that fraction to the session's `session_checkpoint_meta` row on every restore (the value on `partial_workspace`, NULL on `full`), and `handoffResumedFrame` (`start.go:3264-3269`, with its caller `synthesizeHandoffReattach` at `events.go:285`) reads that row instead of hard-coding `full := 1.0`, keeping `1.0` when no fraction is recorded, which is what `spec/07:138`, `spec/10:392`, and `spec/15:1421` already require and what no code produces today), C7 (`classifyResume` (`:3214-3219`) gains the `spec/04:264` precedence rule: the eviction-state row wins only when the resume restored nothing, which is the snapshotless-rebuild path `SelectForResume`'s `ErrNotFound` selects, so any restorable checkpoint beats it. `HasEvictionState` keeps its boolean signature and `Options.EvictionStateLookup` is assigned in `cmd/` for the first time).
- `pkg/gateway/runtime/adapterclient/client.go`: P1 and C2 (streaming `Checkpoint` and `Resume` clients, both slot-qualified and both stamping the intent row's `coordination_generation`, which `Client.Checkpoint` (`:466-471`) does not send today while `CheckpointBarrier` (`:499-502`) does; `CheckpointBarrier` carries the gateway-minted id and the slot).
- `pkg/gateway/runtime/adapterclient/coordinatorfence.go` and `pkg/gateway/coordination/coordfence/coordfence.go`: P1, C1, and C7 (the fence's sender path carries the slot. `Client.CoordinatorFence` (`coordinatorfence.go:48-54`) builds `CoordinatorFenceRequest` with `SessionId` and `CoordinationGeneration` alone and now stamps the `slot_id` P1 adds; `coordfence.FenceClient` (`:62-64`), `Fencer.Fence` (`:137`), and `fence` (`:143`) carry the slot through the retry/relinquish loop; and the fence's two gateway callers, `sessionserver.CoordinationFencer` (`sessionserver.go:822`) and `fenceResumedPod` (`start.go:3176-3180`), pass the session's slot, which is empty on a single-session pool. Without the sender the field arrives empty, `useSlot` (`pkg/adapter/slot.go:39-41`) takes the adapter's pod-global branch on every fence, the per-slot `lastFenced` state C1 adds is never written, and C7's handoff on a `maxConcurrentSessions > 1` pod fails at the fence and drops every slot to the minimal-state fallback).
- `pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go`: C2 (`Binder.Resume` calls the restore driver at `:1539`; `ResumeRequest` carries the selected row's `checkpoint_id` and its `slot_id`, which the driver puts on `ResumeStart` so the adapter extracts against the slot's workspace root; `ResumeResult` gains `RestoreMode` and `RecoveryFraction`), C7 (a new exported `AdoptForCheckpoint(ctx, sandboxName, sessionID, slotID) (*BindResult, error)` built on the `resolveSandbox` (`:1908-1917`) and `DialAdapter` (`:56-58`) pair the unexported `reconnect` (`:1096-1100`) already uses. It is the `NotifyPodTerminating` handler's only route to the terminating pod's adapter, which `CoordinatorFence` (`coordfence.go:137`) takes as a parameter and `Checkpointer.snapshot` reads off the registry binding (`checkpointer.go:237-241`). It claims no pod, assigns no slot, mints no credentials, and starts no session. The handler pairs it with `Registry.Put` and `Registry.Remove` (`registry.go`) so every binding it creates is retired and every client it dialed is closed when the RPC returns).
- `pkg/gateway/sessionserver/derive.go`, `replay.go`, `session_subresources.go`: P1 and C2 (the three single-object `WorkspaceSnapshot.Ref` readers become prefix readers backed by a manifest row).
- `pkg/blobstore/blobstore.go`, `pkg/blobstore/miniostore/miniostore.go`, `pkg/blobstore/cataloging/cataloging.go`, `pkg/blobstore/s3/s3.go`, `pkg/blobstore/gcs/gcs.go`, `pkg/blobstore/azureblob/azureblob.go`, `pkg/blobstore/fsstore.go`: C2 (`TenantScoped.SoftDelete(u, retention)` forwarding method with the existing `check(u)` tenant guard, plus the context-carrying `PutContext` / `GetContext` / `SoftDeleteContext` interface the checkpoint drivers use, with `PutContext` treating an `ErrConflict` on the chunk's own key as success when `Stat` reports the byte length being written. The interface is implemented on **every** backend `blobproviderflags.Resolve` can return (`pkg/blobstore/providerflags/providerflags.go:105-150`: `miniostore`, `MemoryStore`, `FilesystemStore`, `s3`, `gcs`, `azureblob`), each threading the caller's context into the SDK call in place of the `context.Background()` it opens today (`s3.go:222-223`), and forwarded through `cataloging`, because the cloud-managed profile (`spec/17:1415`, `:1447`) is a first-class deployment and a MinIO-only surface would leave the checkpoint path with either no `Put` at all or an uncancellable one there. `cmd/lenny-gateway/stores.go` asserts the surface on the resolved store at startup and fails closed when it is absent. The three verbs of the `Store` interface are unchanged and every existing caller keeps them).
- `pkg/gateway/session/sessioncheckpointmeta/sessioncheckpointmeta.go` and its `pgstore`: C6 (a narrow `SetWorkspaceRecoveryFraction(ctx, tenantID, sessionID, fraction *float64)` writer on the `Store` interface, on `MemoryStore`, and on the pgstore, which writes the `workspace_recovery_fraction` column alone. The shipped `Upsert` replaces the whole `(tenant, session)` row and would clobber the barrier's `barrier_id` and `checkpoint_ref`, and the column has no producer today: the barrier ack is its only writer and carries no fraction (`barrier.go:203`, `wiring.go:58-60`, `schemas/lenny-adapter.proto:1176-1180`), so `migrations/0148_session_checkpoint_meta.up.sql:44` is always NULL and the coordinator-handoff frame hard-codes `1.0`).
- `pkg/gateway/coordination/barrier/barrier.go` and `wiring.go`, `pkg/gateway/session/sessioncheckpointmeta/sessioncheckpointmeta.go`: C1 and C2 (`Dispatcher.Send` and `adapterclient.Client.CheckpointBarrier` carry the gateway-minted `checkpoint_id` and the slot; `Coordinator.dispatchOne` commits the intent row before `Send`, dispatches with an empty id when that commit fails, and drives the upload driver on ack; `Coordinator.Dispatch` (`barrier.go:159-178`) groups its targets by `PodAddr` and holds a per-pod gate over the on-ack flush, because a `Target` is one session (`barrier.go:57-69`, `wiring.go:112-135`) and the several sessions of a concurrent-session pod would otherwise open concurrent `Checkpoint` streams into one adapter, whose operation lock is pod-global (`pkg/adapter/server.go:328`) and aborts all but two of them; the barrier `Send` and its ack stay concurrent across every target, so quiescence still fans out in parallel; the `Metrics` interface (`barrier.go:100-101`), whose only method today is `IncPreStopBarrierTargetSource`, gains `IncCheckpointBarrierAck(pool, outcome)` and the ack-duration observation, which is where `lenny_checkpoint_barrier_ack_total` and `lenny_checkpoint_barrier_ack_duration_seconds` get their first emitter).
- `pkg/alerting/rules/rules.go` (`:348-355`, `:358-366`) and `pkg/observability/metrics/catalog.go` (`:289`): S1 (the single-sourced `CheckpointStorageUnavailable` `Summary` and `Description` stop scoping the alert to eviction and stop promising a Postgres minimal-state fallback on every failure; its `Expr` (`:350`) gains the `reason!~"manifest_write_failed|no_chunks_committed"` selector, so the two non-object-store increment conditions this change adds cannot page on-call with a MinIO headline or flip the `objectStore` health component to `unhealthy` (`pkg/alerting/rules/health_components.go:61`, `spec/25:448`) while MinIO is healthy; the `MinIOUnavailable` `Description` (`:363`) stops naming a pod replica as an `ArtifactStore` PUT issuer with its own retry budget, which is the bucket-A site the agent pod no longer has a path for, and attributes both to the gateway; the counter's catalog help text stops scoping it to non-eviction checkpoints. Both alerts' names, severities, expressions, and runbook targets are unchanged).
- `cmd/lenny-gateway/stores.go`, `httpsurface.go`, `sessionsrv.go`, `main.go`, `workers.go`: C2 (tenant-scoped wiring of the cataloged store into the drivers, barrier-dispatch wiring, the `checkpoint_manifest` deleter on the retention GC), C4 (`checkpoint_manifest` store construction, `hardPrunePartialManifests` re-key, and the §12.5 backstop sweep built as a second leader-gated entry beside `tombstone-hard-prune` at `workers.go:803-841`), C5 (`prestop.CheckpointFnFor` at `httpsurface.go:617` carries the eviction trigger), C6 (no edit needed for `Options.PartialManifestLookup`, which `cmd/` never assigns), C7 (the `evictionfallback.Writer` and `Options.EvictionStateLookup` are wired here for the first time, and the eviction-checkpoint component the `NotifyPodTerminating` method delegates to is passed into `newGatewayControlServer` (`main.go:582`) from `controlserver.go:161`, carrying the `*podsession.Binder` the wiring struct already holds (`wiring_fields.go:308`) so the handler can dial the terminating pod; the three new checkpoint flags are parsed). `httpsurface.go` and `sessionsrv.go` carry no `GatewayControl` reference today and gain none.
- `migrations/0062_session_partial_checkpoint_manifest.up.sql` and `.down.sql`, `migrations/0150_partial_manifest_active_uniq.up.sql`: C4 (rewritten in place as `checkpoint_manifest` and its corrected indexes).
- `pkg/checkpoint/checkpoint.go`: C5 and C7 (`RetryBudgetFor` gains its first non-test callers; the `Trigger` enum and every signature are unchanged).

All are rewritten per §6.

All five apply service-mode templates, whose floor drops from the shipped 840s to the two-term 20s under the mode split: `TestDecideTemplate_TerminationGraceFloor_spec_5_2_516` (`pkg/admission/pool_config_validator/validator_test.go:308`, which pins `maxConcurrent × tier_cap + checkpointBarrierAckTimeoutSeconds + 30`); `TestDecideTemplateRejectsBelowGraceFloor_spec_5_2_516` (`tests/tier2_component/admission/pool_config_validator_test.go:182`, a service-mode template with an explicit 120s grace period that the 20s service-mode floor now admits, rewritten onto a session-mode template with `sessionPolicy.maxConcurrentSessions: 8` so it keeps its rejection against the 1220s floor); and `TestPoolConfigValidatorPropagatesTerminationGraceWarning_spec_5_2_516` (`pkg/admission/webhook/pool_config_validator_test.go:191`, a grace-less service-mode `maxConcurrent: 8` template that flips from admit-with-one-warning to admit-with-no-warning against its 20s floor, rewritten onto a session-mode template whose 1220s floor is above the 600s warning threshold so the warning it pins still fires); `TestPoolConfigValidatorRejectsTerminationGraceCeilingBreach_spec_5_2_516` (`pkg/admission/webhook/pool_config_validator_test.go:215`, a service-mode `maxConcurrent: 8` template with `maxTerminationGracePeriodSeconds: 600`, whose shipped 840s floor breaches the ceiling and whose 20s service-mode floor does not, so its rejection, its 422, and its `spec.maxTerminationGracePeriodSeconds (600s)` message assertions invert; rewritten onto a session-mode template with `sessionPolicy.maxConcurrentSessions: 8`, whose 1220s floor breaches the same ceiling); and the `"maxTerminationGracePeriodSeconds breach sets BudgetExceeded"` subtest of `TestDecideTemplate_BudgetExceededDiscriminator_spec_10_1_129` (`pkg/admission/pool_config_validator/validator_test.go:479-489`, the same service-mode ceiling breach, asserting the rejection reason and `d.BudgetExceeded`; re-keyed the same way so the discriminator it pins survives). The `"service mode carries no pool-config invariant"` case of `TestTemplate` (`validator_test.go:232`, `executionMode: service, maxConcurrent: 4` with no grace period) **keeps** its admit expectation under the 20s service-mode floor and is the case that pins the mode split, so it is not a rewrite.
- Rewrites (the 180s podspec default). Six shipped tests assert the 120s default or the `--timeout=110s` that follows from it and are rewritten with C7: `TestBuildDefaultsTerminationGraceTo120_spec_4_6_1` (`pkg/controller/sandbox/podspec/podspec_test.go:440`), `TestBuildIgnoresCeilingAboveDefault_spec_4_6_1` (`:469`, which yields 180 under C7), `TestBuildAddsPreStopDrainHook_spec_4_6_1` (`:487`, whose case table covers only the `adapter` and embedded containers and never the sidecar `runtime` container), the `"zero base ignored"` case of `TestBuildAppliesTerminationGraceBaseOverride_spec_5_2` (`:540`), the `"preconnect default grace unaffected"` case of `TestBuildPreConnectFloorsTerminationGrace_spec_6_1_67` (`:572`), and `TestReconcileCreatesPodForNewSandbox` (`pkg/controller/sandbox/controller_test.go:347`, which asserts the rendered pod carries `terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 120`). `pkg/controller/sandbox/controller_test.go` is the only file outside `podspec/` that the default reaches.
- Rewrites (the `checkpoint_manifest` rename and the gateway-side I/O). Eight further shipped tests assert the surfaces this change replaces and are rewritten with them, per §6. Three of them are the unit suites of the packages the re-key lands in, and each takes its own package's build down: `pkg/gateway/checkpoint/partialmanifeststore/partialmanifeststore_test.go` (the store's own suite, which builds `Record{… Generation: 7, PartialObjectKeyPrefix: …}` at `:22-27`, calls the generation-keyed `Get` at `:31`, and pins the pre-change resume selector in `TestLatestActiveReturnsHighestGeneration` at `:96`; rewritten onto the `(tenant_id, checkpoint_id)` key, `object_key_prefix`, and the `SelectForResume` / `SelectLatestFullBefore` / `ListSweepable` selectors, with `LatestActive` re-scoped to `partial = TRUE`); `pkg/gateway/checkpoint/checkpointer/partialcleanup_test.go` (the cleaner's own suite, whose `stubChunkDeleter` at `:15-22` implements the `DeleteByPrefix` method C2 deletes and whose success case asserts deletion by prefix at `:71-72`; rewritten onto the per-key `SoftDelete` surface over the derived key set, with `TestCleanupPartialManifestNilDeleterSkipsMinIO` at `:146` deleted because the nil-deleter skip it pins is T-4.4.16); and `cmd/lenny-gateway/main_test.go` (`TestHardPrunePartialManifests` at `:657`, which drives `hardPrunePartialManifests` against generation-keyed `MemoryStore` rows; rewritten onto the re-keyed store and the reason-agnostic hard-prune predicate). The remaining five are: `tests/tier2_component/migrations/prod_columns_test.go` (`:164` and the `0150` comment at `:421-422`, which name `session_partial_checkpoint_manifest` and drive `TestProdMigrationsApplyExpectedSchema` and `TestProdMigrationsRollBackPerStep` against a real Postgres, so both fail on the first run after C4's in-place rename; the same file additionally gains a `prodMigrationSchema` row for S3's new `termination_grace_period_seconds` column on `sandbox_warm_pools`, without which `scripts/lint-migrations.sh:87-88` fails the build because no test references the new migration number); `tests/tier2_component/stores/partialmanifeststore_test.go` (`:31`, `TestPartialManifestStoreContract`, which pins the `(tenant_id, session_id, generation)` row key and the `deleted_at IS NULL`-only `LatestActive` predicate); `tests/tier2_component/stores/partialcleanup_component_test.go` (`:35-46`, whose `minioPrefixDeleter` implements the `DeleteByPrefix` surface C2 removes and issues `ListObjects` plus raw `RemoveObject` calls); `tests/tier4_integration/checkpoint_minio_atomicity_test.go` (`:153`, `:296`, which assign the deleted `Server.Checkpoints` and `Server.Restorer` fields so the file stops compiling after C1, and whose two assertions — the metadata record following the MinIO upload, and a failed upload leaving no record — are both inverted by the intent-row-first ordering; their `tests/spec-map.json:230-231` entries are updated with them); and `pkg/gateway/sessionserver/resumed_event_test.go` (`:52-145`, `TestResumeEmitsPartialWorkspaceWhenPartialManifestPresent` and its `fakePartialLookup`, which construct the `Options.PartialManifestLookup` field C6 deletes, plus `TestResumeEmitsConversationOnlyWhenEvictionStatePresent` at `:92`, rewritten against C7's precedence rule).
- Rewrites (the PoolScalingController mirror). `TestPoolStoreSourceLeavesSessionPolicyNilWithoutScrubControl` (`pkg/controller/poolscaling/poolstoresource_test.go:263`) asserts `SessionPolicy = nil` for a session-mode pool with no scrub control, which S3's `sessionPolicyToCRD` mirror falsifies for any pool carrying `maxConcurrentSessions`. It is rewritten, and §6 adds the mapper assertion on `PoolStoreSource.ListPoolConfigs` that the mirror actually renders the field.

- `PROPOSAL-QUEUE.md:55` (queue item C-01, "does the pod-side adapter talk to MinIO directly (requiring §13 pod-side credential/egress delivery) or stream to the gateway, and what are the object-key layout, the atomic metadata/manifest record, and the eviction-trigger mechanism"): this proposal is that item's resolution on all four axes. Close the row.

## 11. Blast-radius sweep coverage

The sweep behind §10 enumerated 108 sites across `spec/`, `docs/`, `schemas/`, `sdks/`, `charts/`, and `TESTING.md`, in eight buckets. Every bucket is folded into a staged change above:

| Bucket | Subject | Where it lands |
|:--|:--|:--|
| A | Adapter assigned object-store I/O | S1, P1, C1 |
| B | One-object-per-checkpoint assumptions | S2, C2, C4 |
| C | Checkpoint object-key layout | S2 (the four-segment grammar at `spec/12:307` is confirmed unchanged) |
| D | `checkpoint_id` provenance | P1, S2 |
| E | Agent-pod egress, credentials, NetworkPolicy | **No change.** Enumerated in §2 and above to record that the pod gains no egress rule, MinIO gains no ingress allow, and the §4.9 lease-provider set gains no object-store provider. The tier-11 assertion in §6 fails the build if any of them changes. |
| F | SDK and client surface | S1, S2, P1 (spec, schema, and doc-page wording only for the runtime SDKs, because the three runtime-SDK lifecycle files already describe the checkpoint handshake as quiesce-only), C2 (the three client SDK session types gain the `failureReason` field the §15.1 envelope gains, which is the field `WORKSPACE_SNAPSHOT_MISSING` travels on) |
| G | preStop and eviction trigger | S3, P1, C5, C7 |
| H | Metrics, alerts, runbooks | S1 (owner and subject changes on the §16.1 inventory rows, their `docs/reference/metrics.md` mirrors, and the runbook pages they route to), C2 (`pkg/gateway/metrics/gatewaymetrics`, where `lenny_checkpoint_partial_total` is registered with one label and has no emitter) |

Three sites in the sweep are **not** folded in, and each is recorded here rather than silently dropped:

- **`spec/12:305`'s 500MB-versus-512MiB drift** and **`spec/16:274`'s label mismatch with `spec/04:287`** are pre-existing defects unrelated to checkpoint ownership. Both are corrected in passing (S2 and S1 respectively) because this change edits the surrounding paragraphs and leaving them would fail the proposal's own tier-11 consistency assertion. They are noted so a reviewer does not mistake them for new breakage.
- **`docs/runbooks/checkpoint-stale.md`'s `checkpoint.maxSizeBytes` and `checkpoint.postgresFallbackMaxBytes`** name two knobs that exist nowhere in `spec/`, `charts/`, or `pkg/`. They are removed from the page (S1) rather than defined, because nothing in this change needs them and inventing two knobs to justify a runbook line is the wrong direction. A third invented name, `checkpointWorker.concurrency`, *is* needed by the gateway-side upload path and is defined as a real Helm value (S2). The page's fourth unfamiliar name, `lenny_checkpoint_size_bytes`, is **not** in this class: it is declared at `spec/16:39` and registered at `pkg/observability/metrics/catalog.go:125`, so its diagnosis step is kept and only its emitter framing changes (S1).
